{"content": "1|\"\"\"Desired-state reconciler — makes ARM's recorded desired state execute safely (§10B/§13).\n2|\n3|Safety model (§8):\n4|- Acts ONLY on Agent Runtime Manager-owned runtimes with verified durable\n5|  ownership markers on the backing VM. Mismatch fails closed.\n6|- desired RUNNING  → observe → start owned VM if stopped → wait for\n7|  bridge readiness (bounded) → actual RUNNING.\n8|- desired STOPPED  → graceful shutdown → verify stopped → NEVER destroy a\n9|  persistent runtime VM as part of reconcile.\n10|- desired DESTROYED → ownership-verified destroy only (explicit API path,\n11|  never an automatic reconcile consequence).\n12|- Recovery: bounded retries with backoff; every attempt audited via\n13|  RuntimeEvent; last_reconcile_at / last_error / recovery_count recorded.\n14|\n15|Single-replica discipline (TDR-0005): one loop instance per process; the API\n16|deployment runs a single uvicorn worker. Leadership election is future work.\n17|\"\"\"\n18|from __future__ import annotations\n19|\n20|import logging\n21|import time\n22|from dataclasses import dataclass, field\n23|from datetime import datetime, timezone\n24|\n25|from sqlalchemy import select\n26|from sqlalchemy.orm import Session as SASession\n27|\n28|from server_manager.agent_runtime_manager.models.orm import (\n29|    ActualState,\n30|    AgentRuntime,\n31|    RuntimeEvent,\n32|    RuntimeState,\n33|)\n34|from server_manager.agent_runtime_manager.providers.proxmox_vm import (\n35|    OwnershipError,\n36|    ProxmoxVMProvider,\n37|    verify_ownership,\n38|)\n39|\n40|log = logging.getLogger(\"arm.reconciler\")\n41|\n42|\n43|@dataclass\n44|class ReconcileSettings:\n45|    \"\"\"Tunables (env-overridable). Backoff is exponential-capped.\"\"\"\n46|\n47|    max_recovery_attempts: int = 3\n48|    backoff_base_s: int = 10\n49|    backoff_cap_s: int = 120\n50|    bridge_wait_s: int = 300\n51|    poll_interval_s: int = 60\n52|    shutdown_wait_s: int = 30\n53|\n54|    @classmethod\n55|    def from_env(cls) -> \"ReconcileSettings\":\n56|        import os\n57|\n58|        def _i(name: str, default: int) -> int:\n59|            try:\n60|                return int(os.environ.get(name, default))\n61|            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n62|                return default\n63|\n64|        return cls(\n65|            max_recovery_attempts=_i(\"ARM_RECONCILE_MAX_ATTEMPTS\", 3),\n66|            backoff_base_s=_i(\"ARM_RECONCILE_BACKOFF_BASE_S\", 10),\n67|            backoff_cap_s=_i(\"ARM_RECONCILE_BACKOFF_CAP_S\", 120),\n68|            bridge_wait_s=_i(\"ARM_RECONCILE_BRIDGE_WAIT_S\", 300),\n69|            poll_interval_s=_i(\"ARM_RECONCILE_POLL_S\", 60),\n70|            shutdown_wait_s=_i(\"ARM_RECONCILE_SHUTDOWN_WAIT_S\", 30),\n71|        )\n72|\n73|\n74|@dataclass\n75|class ReconcileResult:\n76|    runtime_id: str\n77|    desired: str\n78|    before: str\n79|    after: str\n80|    action: str  # noop | started | shutdown | wait_bridge | error | skipped_unowned\n81|    error: str | None = None\n82|    detail: dict = field(default_factory=dict)\n83|\n84|\n85|class ReconciliationService:\n86|    \"\"\"One reconcile pass over ARM-owned runtimes (or a single runtime).\"\"\"\n87|\n88|    def __init__(self, provider: ProxmoxVMProvider | None = None, settings: ReconcileSettings | None = None):\n89|        self.provider = provider or ProxmoxVMProvider()\n90|        self.settings = settings or ReconcileSettings.from_env()\n91|\n92|    # ------------------------------------------------------------- helpers\n93|    @staticmethod\n94|    def _now() -> datetime:\n95|        return datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n96|\n97|    def _pve_vm_state(self, node: str | None, vmid: int | None) -> str:\n98|        \"\"\"True PVE state: 'running' | 'stopped' | 'absent' | 'unknown'.\"\"\"\n99|        if not node or vmid is None:\n100|            return \"absent\"\n101|        try:\n102|            st = self.provider.vm_status(node, vmid) or {}\n103|            return st.get(\"status\") or \"unknown\"\n104|        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — PVE unreachable is a recorded condition\n105|            log.warning(\"pve state read failed for %s/%s: %s\", node, vmid, e)\n106|            return \"unknown\"\n107|\n108|    def _wait_pve_state(self, node: str | None, vmid: int | None, expect: str, max_s: int = 30) -> str:\n109|        \"\"\"Bounded poll for a PVE state transition (async state flips lag the API ack).\"\"\"\n110|        deadline = time.time() + max_s\n111|        state = self._pve_vm_state(node, vmid)\n112|        while state != expect and time.time() < deadline:\n113|            time.sleep(3)\n114|            state = self._pve_vm_state(node, vmid)\n115|        return state\n116|\n117|    def _verify_owned(self, session: SASession, r: AgentRuntime) -> None:\n118|        \"\"\"Fail-closed ownership verification before ANY state-changing action.\"\"\"\n119|        if r.node is None or r.vmid is None:\n120|            raise OwnershipError(f\"runtime {r.runtime_id} has no backing VM (node/vmid null)\")\n121|        s = __import__(\"server_manager.common.config.settings\", fromlist=[\"get_settings\"]).get_settings()\n122|        verify_ownership(self.provider, r.node, r.vmid, r, s.protected_vmids)\n123|\n124|    def _audit(self, session: SASession, r: AgentRuntime, action: str, result: str, detail: dict) -> None:\n125|        session.add(RuntimeEvent(actor=\"arm-reconciler\", action=action, runtime_id=r.runtime_id,\n126|                                 result=result, detail=detail))\n127|\n128|    # ------------------------------------------------------------- pass\n129|    def reconcile_runtime(self, session: SASession, runtime_id) -> ReconcileResult:\n130|        \"\"\"Reconcile a single runtime by id. Commit is the caller's responsibility.\"\"\"\n131|        r = session.get(AgentRuntime, runtime_id)\n132|        if r is None:\n133|            return ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(runtime_id), desired=\"?\", before=\"?\", after=\"?\",\n134|                                   action=\"error\", error=\"runtime not found\")\n135|        before = r.actual_state.value if r.actual_state else ActualState.ABSENT.value\n136|        desired = r.desired_state\n137|\n138|        # SUPERSEDED rows are historical evidence (failed provisioning attempts\n139|        # linked to a live retry) — evidence-preserving, never reconciled, never\n140|        # flipped back by PVE truth-correction.\n141|        if r.actual_state == ActualState.SUPERSEDED:\n142|            res = ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(r.runtime_id), desired=desired.value, before=before,\n143|                                  after=before, action=\"noop\",\n144|                                  detail={\"note\": \"superseded rows are historical; reconciler skips\"})\n145|            self._record(session, r, res)\n146|            return res\n147|\n148|        pve = self._pve_vm_state(r.node, r.vmid)\n149|\n150|        # desired DESTROYED is handled only by the explicit API destroy path (§8);\n151|        # the reconciler must not auto-destroy.\n152|        if desired == RuntimeState.DESIRED_DESTROYED:\n153|            res = ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(r.runtime_id), desired=desired.value, before=before,\n154|                                  after=before, action=\"noop\",\n155|                                  detail={\"note\": \"destroy is API-only, reconciler never auto-destroys\"})\n156|            self._record(session, r, res)\n157|            return res\n158|\n159|        # PVE unreachable: record and stop safely (no state flip on unknown ground truth)\n160|        if pve == \"unknown\":\n161|            res = ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(r.runtime_id), desired=desired.value, before=before,\n162|                                  after=before, action=\"error\", error=\"PVE unavailable\")\n163|            r.last_error = \"PVE unavailable at reconcile\"\n164|            self._record(session, r, res)\n165|            return res\n166|\n167|        try:\n168|            if desired == RuntimeState.DESIRED_RUNNING:\n169|                res = self._reconcile_running(session, r, before, pve)\n170|            else:  # DESIRED_STOPPED\n171|                res = self._reconcile_stopped(session, r, before, pve)\n172|        except OwnershipError as e:\n173|            res = ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(r.runtime_id), desired=desired.value, before=before,\n174|                                  after=before, action=\"error\", error=f\"ownership: {e}\")\n175|            self._audit(session, r, \"reconcile_refused\", \"refused\", {\"reason\": str(e)})\n176|        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — recorded as reconcile failure, never crash the pass\n177|            log.exception(\"reconcile failed for %s\", r.runtime_id)\n178|            res = ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(r.runtime_id), desired=desired.value, before=before,\n179|                                  after=before, action=\"error\", error=f\"{e.__class__.__name__}: {e}\"[:500])\n180|            self._audit(session, r, \"reconcile_failed\", \"error\", {\"error\": res.error})\n181|        self._record(session, r, res)\n182|        return res\n183|\n184|    # ------------------------------------------------- desired RUNNING\n185|    def _reconcile_running(self, session: SASession, r: AgentRuntime, before: str, pve: str) -> ReconcileResult:\n186|        if pve == \"running\":\n187|            # Already up: derive truth, clear error only if it was PVE-related.\n188|            if r.actual_state != ActualState.RUNNING:\n189|                r.actual_state = ActualState.RUNNING\n190|                r.recovery_count = 0\n191|                r.last_error = None\n192|                self._audit(session, r, \"reconcile_observe\", \"ok\",\n193|                            {\"note\": \"PVE says running; actual corrected\", \"pve\": pve})\n194|            return ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(r.runtime_id), desired=\"DESIRED_RUNNING\",\n195|                                   before=before, after=ActualState.RUNNING.value, action=\"noop\")\n196|        if pve not in (\"stopped\",):\n197|            # 'absent' with desired RUNNING → provisioning job territory (not reconcile)\n198|            return ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(r.runtime_id), desired=\"DESIRED_RUNNING\",\n199|                                   before=before, after=before, action=\"noop\",\n200|                                   detail={\"note\": f\"VM pve_state={pve}; provisioning path owns this state\"})\n201|\n202|        # stopped → must start, but ONLY an owned VM\n203|        self._verify_owned(session, r)\n204|        if r.recovery_count >= self.settings.max_recovery_attempts:\n205|            r.last_error = f\"recovery exhausted ({r.recovery_count} attempts)\"\n206|            self._audit(session, r, \"reconcile_recovery_exhausted\", \"error\",\n207|                        {\"attempts\": r.recovery_count})\n208|            return ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(r.runtime_id), desired=\"DESIRED_RUNNING\",\n209|                                   before=before, after=before, action=\"error\",\n210|                                   error=r.last_error, detail={\"recovery_count\": r.recovery_count})\n211|        # bounded backoff before a start retry\n212|        if r.recovery_count > 0:\n213|            delay = min(self.settings.backoff_base_s * (2 ** (r.recovery_count - 1)),\n214|                        self.settings.backoff_cap_s)\n215|            self._audit(session, r, \"reconcile_backoff\", \"ok\", {\"delay_s\": delay})\n216|            # In-loop sleep is bounded by cap (≤120s); single-threaded loop accepts it.\n217|            time.sleep(delay)\n218|        self._audit(session, r, \"reconcile_start\", \"ok\", {\"node\": r.node, \"vmid\": r.vmid})\n219|        self.provider.start(r.node, r.vmid)\n220|        r.actual_state = ActualState.RUNNING\n221|        r.recovery_count = (r.recovery_count or 0) + 1\n222|        r.last_error = None\n223|        return ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(r.runtime_id), desired=\"DESIRED_RUNNING\",\n224|                               before=before, after=ActualState.RUNNING.value, action=\"started\",\n225|                               detail={\"node\": r.node, \"vmid\": r.vmid})\n226|\n227|    # ------------------------------------------------ desired STOPPED\n228|    def _reconcile_stopped(self, session: SASession, r: AgentRuntime, before: str, pve: str) -> ReconcileResult:\n229|        if pve == \"stopped\":\n230|            if r.actual_state != ActualState.STOPPED:\n231|                r.actual_state = ActualState.STOPPED\n232|                self._audit(session, r, \"reconcile_observe\", \"ok\",\n233|                            {\"note\": \"PVE says stopped; actual corrected\", \"pve\": pve})\n234|            return ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(r.runtime_id), desired=\"DESIRED_STOPPED\",\n235|                                   before=before, after=ActualState.STOPPED.value, action=\"noop\")\n236|        if pve == \"absent\":\n237|            return ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(r.runtime_id), desired=\"DESIRED_STOPPED\",\n238|                                   before=before, after=before, action=\"noop\",\n239|                                   detail={\"note\": \"no backing VM\"})\n240|        # running → graceful shutdown (NEVER destroy)\n241|        self._verify_owned(session, r)\n242|        self._audit(session, r, \"reconcile_shutdown\", \"ok\", {\"node\": r.node, \"vmid\": r.vmid})\n243|        self.provider.shutdown(r.node, r.vmid)  # escalates to forced stop internally on timeout\n244|        after = self._wait_pve_state(r.node, r.vmid, \"stopped\", max_s=self.settings.shutdown_wait_s)\n245|        if after == \"stopped\":\n246|            r.actual_state = ActualState.STOPPED\n247|            r.recovery_count = 0\n248|            r.last_error = None\n249|            return ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(r.runtime_id), desired=\"DESIRED_STOPPED\",\n250|                                   before=before, after=ActualState.STOPPED.value, action=\"shutdown\")\n251|        r.last_error = f\"shutdown did not converge (pve={after})\"\n252|        self._audit(session, r, \"reconcile_shutdown_unverified\", \"error\", {\"pve\": after})\n253|        return ReconcileResult(runtime_id=str(r.runtime_id), desired=\"DESIRED_STOPPED\",\n254|                               before=before, after=before, action=\"error\", error=r.last_error)\n255|\n256|    # --------------------------------------------------------- recording\n257|    def _record(self, session: SASession, r: AgentRuntime, res: ReconcileResult) -> None:\n258|        r.last_reconcile_at = self._now()\n259|        if res.action == \"error\":\n260|            r.last_error = res.error\n261|        elif res.action in (\"noop\", \"started\", \"shutdown\") and res.after and res.desired:\n262|            # converged: desired RUNNING→actual RUNNING, DESIRED_STOPPED→STOPPED\n263|            expected = \"RUNNING\" if res.desired == \"DESIRED_RUNNING\" else \"STOPPED\"\n264|            if res.after == expected:\n265|                r.last_error = None\n266|        session.flush()\n267|\n268|    # {"content": "1|# PROBLEM TO SOLVE: Work Item ↔ Session boundary semantics\n2|\n3|**Status:** OPEN — human decision required\n4|**Raised:** 2026-09-28 (STEA-004 REV2 plan §5, from the human worksheet)\n5|**Blocks:** eventual hardening of REQ-055; no production behavior is changed by this document.\n6|\n7|## The conflict\n8|\n9|The live multi-session proof (2026-09-28, prod) treats **one Work Item as the durable\n10|unit that spans many disposable execution sessions**: Session 1 closes, Session 2\n11|opens with a compact reconstructed context package, and the same Work Item stays\n12|ACTIVE with its assignment intact. This is a literal reading of ACMS-REQ-055\n13|(\"continuity across disposable sessions\").\n14|\n15|The human's clarified preferred operating workflow (2026-09-28 worksheet) is:\n16|\n17|```text\n18|measurable goal → fresh session → handoff → create a NEW plan → /new → next measurable goal\n19|```\n20|\n21|…which semantically maps better to: **each fresh session gets a NEW Work Item**, with\n22|the durable continuity carried by a parent (Feature/Program) rather than by one\n23|long-lived Work Item. The human explicitly does NOT want the already-working\n24|multi-session capability removed.\n25|\n26|## Options\n27|\n28|### A. One Work Item spans many sessions (current implementation)\n29|\n30|- Work = long-lived unit of accountability; sessions = disposable reasoning contexts.\n31|- Budgets (REQ-059) naturally attach to the whole multi-session effort.\n32|- Matches the shipped Phase 8 proof; no migration of concepts.\n33|- Risk: Work Items accumulate unrelated sessions; \"done\" is per-session handoffs,\n34|  so Work-level completion criteria get fuzzy.\n35|\n36|### B. Parent Feature/Program spans many Work Items; each new session normally gets a new Work Item\n37|\n38|- Work Item ≈ one measurable goal/execution sprint; parent carries continuity.\n39|- Clean 1:1 mapping between session runs and Work completion; budgets attach at\n40|  either level (parent = program envelope, child = sprint envelope).\n41|- Requires: convention (or enforcement) that rotation closes the child Work and\n42|  creates a sibling under the same parent, plus rollups that aggregate children\n43|  into the parent.\n44|- Risk: more bookkeeping; the Phase 8 proof flow becomes the \"exception\" path.\n45|\n46|### C. Support both; B is the default\n47|\n48|- Keep A working (additive, already deployed), make B the recommended pattern.\n49|- Requires the parent-child rollups of B plus documentation; no destructive change.\n50|- Risk: two patterns can coexist confusingly without clear guidance about when\n51|  to use each.\n52|\n53|## What is NOT in question\n54|\n55|- Sessions stay disposable; checkpoints/handoffs remain the continuity mechanism.\n56|- Budgets and telemetry stay per-Work and per-session as implemented in v0.9.0.\n57|- No retention/purge behavior changes.\n58|\n59|## Recommendation (agent view, non-binding)\n60|\n61|Option C: the deployed implementation is additive and correct for long-running\n62|efforts (e.g. this infrastructure program itself), while B matches the\n63|human's daily build-measure-rotate loop. Decide which pattern the A2A delivery\n64|slice should *enforce* vs merely *permit*.\n65|\n66|## Decision needed from the human\n67|\n68|1. Which option is canonical (A / B / C-with-B-default)?\n69|2. If B: should session rotation auto-close the child Work Item and open a sibling,\n70|   or leave that to the operator?\n71|", "total_lines": 70, "file_size": 3213, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

\"critical\"\n83|    if severity and sev != severity:\n84|        return None\n85|    return {\n86|        \"event_id\": r.event_id,\n87|        \"sequence\": r.sequence,\n88|        \"timestamp\": attempt 2 of 3 proceeds (after a 0s backoff in tests)\n224|    assert res.action == \"started\"\n225|    assert r.recovery_count == 2\n226|\n227|\n228|def test_reconciler_never_auto_destroys(arm_session, recon):\n229|    svc, fake = recon\n230|    r = make_runtime(arm_session, desired_state=RuntimeState.DESIRED_DESTROYED,\n231|                     actual_state=ActualState.RUNNING)\n232|    fake.pve_state = \"running\"\n233|    res = svc.reconcile_runtime(arm_session, r.runtime_id)\n234|    assert res.action == \"noop\"\n235|    assert fake.calls and all(c[0] in (\"vm_status\",) for c in fake.calls)\n236|\n237|\n238|def test_shutdown_nonconvergence_recorded(arm_session, recon):\n239|    svc, fake = recon\n240|    fake.pve_state = \"running\"\n241|    fake.shutdown_fails_to_stop = True\n242|    r = make_runtime(arm_session, desired_state=RuntimeState.DESIRED_STOPPED,\n243|                     actual_state=ActualState.RUNNING)\n244|    bind_marker(fake, r)\n245|    res = svc.reconcile_runtime(arm_session, r.runtime_id)\n246|    assert res.action == \"error\"\n247|    assert \"did not converge\" in res.error\n248|    assert r.actual_state == ActualState.RUNNING  # honest: not verified stopped\n249|\n250|\n251|def test_observe_corrects_stale_actual(arm_session, recon):\n252|    svc, fake = recon\n253|    # DB thinks STOPPED but PVE says running → corrected to RUNNING, no action\n254|    r = make_runtime(arm_session, actual_state=ActualState.STOPPED)\n255|    fake.pve_state = \"running\"\n256|    res = svc.reconcile_runtime(arm_session, r.runtime_id)\n257|    assert res.action == \"noop\"\n258|    assert r.actual_state == ActualState.RUNNING\n259|\n260|\n261|def test_reconcile_all_pass(arm_session, recon):\n262|    svc, fake = recon\n263|    fake.pve_state = \"stopped\"\n264|    r1 = make_runtime(arm_session, vmid=4242)\n265|    r2 = make_runtime(arm_session, desired_state=RuntimeState.DESIRED_STOPPED, vmid=4243)\n266|    bind_marker(fake, r1)\n267|    bind_marker(fake, r2)\n268|    results = svc.reconcile_all(arm_session)\n269|    actions = {x.runtime_id: x.action for x in results}\n270|    assert actions[str(r1.runtime_id)] == \"started\"\n271|    # r1's start flipped the shared fake PVE state to running; r2 (desired STOPPED) must then shut it down.\n272|    assert actions[str(r2.runtime_id)] == \"shutdown\"\n273|    assert r1.last_reconcile_at is not None\n274|    assert r2.last_reconcile_at is not None\n275|\n276|\n277|# ----------------------------------------------------------------- API layer\n278|def test_api_reconcile_endpoint(arm_client, arm_session, recon, auth_headers, add_token, monkeypatch):\n279|    add_token(\"svc-recon-test\", \"tok-recon-1\", \"ALL\")\n280|    svc, fake = recon\n281|    # {"status": "success", "output": "## Layout this implements\n\n```\n/opt/llm-manager/\n├── releases/<git-sha>/        # immutable release trees (app, collectors, control, ops)\n├── current -> releases/<sha>  # atomic-ish promotion via symlink swap\n└── shared/                    # logs, state that survives releases\n\n/etc/llm-manager/              # mutable + secrets — NEVER inside a release\n├── secrets/                   # values live only on the host (see secret contract below)\n├── tls/\n├── litellm.env\n└── litellm_config.yaml        # GENERATED runtime state (litellm_sync.py) — never blind-overwritten\n```\n\n## Scripts\n\n| Script | Purpose |\n|---|---|\n| `build-release.sh` | Build `llm-manager-<sha>.tar.gz` from a Git worktree; excludes secrets/venvs/logs/DB data; writes `release_manifest.json` with SHA + checksums |\n| `bootstrap-vm.sh` | Idempotent fresh-Ubuntu-24.04 install: packages, Python 3.12, release-specific venv from pinned freeze, systemd units, nginx site, directories. **No secrets embedded** — secrets are supplied out-of-band per the contract |\n| `preflight.sh` | Verify artifact checksum, disk space, current release, systemd/nginx state, DB/LLDAP reachability, current `/healthz` |\n| `deploy-release.sh` | The release transaction: preflight → stop recovery → install release → venv → candidate validation → migrations → activate → restart services (recovery LAST) → smoke → commit or auto-rollback |\n| `healthcheck.sh` | Post-deploy acceptance: units active, `/healthz`, `/v1/models`, history/benchmark APIs, nginx TLS, optional model completion |\n| `rollback.sh` | Application-only rollback to previous release (symlink swap + restart, recovery last). Refuses DB restore — DB changes are forward-only via migrations |\n| `verify-parity.sh` | Compare a target host's deployed tree + configs against a release artifact (md5-based) |\n\n## Migration runner\n\n`db/migrations/` holds numbered, forward-only SQL files plus `migrate.py`\n(ledger table `schema_migrations`: version, checksum, applied_at, git_sha).\n`schema.sql` remains the clean-install reference only.\n\n## Secret contract (names only — values never enter Git or CI)\n\n`/etc/llm-manager/secrets/`: `session_key`, `litellm_master_key`, `ldap_bind_dn`,\n`ldap_bind_pw`, `ldap_base_dn`, `pg_app_creds` (format `PG_PW=<pw>` line),\n`pve_token`, `service_token`, `openrouter_api_key`, `emporia_creds`,\n`litellm_salt`, `keys/` (ssh key for recovery remote ops).\n`/etc/llm-manager/litellm.env`: `OPENROUTER_API_KEY=<value>`.\n\n", "tool_calls_made": 0, "duration_seconds": 0.02}

{"status": "success", "output": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# build-release.sh — build an immutable LLM Manager release artifact from a Git worktree.\n# Usage: build-release.sh [worktree-dir] [output-dir]\n# Produces: llm-manager-<git-sha>.tar.gz + llm-manager-<git-sha>.tar.gz.sha256\n# Excludes: secrets, venvs, logs, DB dumps/contents, caches, .git\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSRC_DIR=\"${1:-$(pwd)}\"\nOUT_DIR=\"${2:-$(pwd)/dist}\"\n\nGIT_SHA=\"$(git -C \"$SRC_DIR\" rev-parse HEAD)\"\nGIT_SHA_SHORT=\"$(git -C \"$SRC_DIR\" rev-parse --short=12 HEAD)\"\nBUILD_TIME=\"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\"\n\necho \"==> Building release for ${GIT_SHA} from ${SRC_DIR}\"\nWORK=\"$(mktemp -d)\"\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$WORK\"' EXIT\nSTAGE=\"$WORK/llm-manager-${GIT_SHA_SHORT}\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$STAGE\"\n# Copy tracked files only — this is the strongest guarantee that the artifact\n# equals reviewed source (untracked local junk can never leak in).\ngit -C \"$SRC_DIR\" archive --format=tar HEAD | tar -x -C \"$STAGE\"\n\n# Release manifest (non-secret build identity)\npython3 - \"$STAGE\" \"$GIT_SHA\" \"$BUILD_TIME\" <<'PY'\nimport hashlib, json, os, sys\nstage, sha, build_time = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]\nfiles = {}\nfor root, dirs, names in os.walk(stage):\n\n", "tool_calls_made": 0, "duration_seconds": 0.02}

Public so tests can drive time deterministically.\"\"\"\n80|        now_dt = self._now_fn()\n81|        async with self._session_factory() as db:\n82|            statuses = await TelemetryService.list_status(db)\n83|            for status in statuses:\n84|                await self._evaluate_agent(db, s, status, now_dt)\n85|            await self._maybe_fleet_reconcile(db, s, now_dt)\n86|\n87|    # ------------------------------------------------------- per-agent logic\n88|\n89|    async def _evaluate_agent(self, db: AsyncSession, s, status: AgentStatusCurrentRecord, now_dt: datetime) -> None:\n90|        agent_id = status.agent_id\n91|        if status.last_contact_at is None:\n92|            return  # never contacted: stays UNKNOWN\n93|\n94|        stale_after = timedelta(seconds=s.heartbeat_stale_seconds)\n95|        reconcile_after = timedelta(seconds=s.stale_reconcile_seconds)\n96|        lc = status.last_contact_at\n97|        if lc.tzinfo is None:\n98|            from datetime import timezone as _tz\n99|            lc = lc.replace(tzinfo=_tz.utc)\n100|        delta = now_dt - lc\n101|\n102|        new_connectivity = status.connectivity\n103|        if delta <= stale_after:\n104|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY\n105|        elif delta >= reconcile_after:\n106|            ok = await self._attempt_reconciliation(db, s, status, now_dt)\n107|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY if ok else CONNECTIVITY_UNREACHABLE\n108|        else:\n109|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_STALE\n110|\n111|        if new_connectivity != status.connectivity:\n112|            prev = status.connectivity\n113|            status.connectivity = new_connectivity\n114|            if new_connectivity == CONNECTIVITY_STALE and status.stale_since is None:\n115|                status.stale_since = now_dt\n116|            elif new_connectivity == CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY:\n117|                status.stale_since = None\n118|            status.updated_at = now_dt\n119|            await add_event(\n120|                db,\n121|                event_type=\"CONNECTIVITY_CHANGED\", actor_source=\"scheduler\", agent_id=agent_id,\n122|                summary=f\"Connectivity {prev} -> {new_connectivity} after {int(delta.total_seconds())}s without contact\",\n123|            )\n124|        await db.commit()\n125|\n126|        # periodic telemetry sample (plan §8)\n127|        last = self._last_sample.get(agent_id)\n128|        if last is None or (now_dt - last).total_seconds() >= s.telemetry_sample_seconds:\n129|            await self._write_sample(db, status, now_dt)\n130|            self._last_sample[agent_id] = now_dt\n131|\n132|    async def _attempt_reconciliation(self, db: AsyncSession, s, status: AgentStatusCurrentRecord, now_dt: datetime) -> bool:\n133|        \"\"\"Request a full status snapshot from the bridge (1 h stale path).\n134|\n135|        Bridge transport lands in Phase 4; until then reconciliation of an\n136|        agent without a live bridge fails (→ UNREACHABLE), which is the honest\n137|        outcome: we cannot reach it.\"\"\"\n138|        await add_event(db, event_type=\"RECONCILIATION_STARTED\",\n139|            actor_source=\"scheduler\", agent_id=status.agent_id,\n140|            summary=f\"Stale >= {s.stale_reconcile_seconds}s - Currently both exist; this could confuse users.\n7. **Retry Behavior**: After an `error` state, should the adapter retry automatically or wait for user action? {"output": "# Current Sprint\n\n## Sprint Goal\n\nCapture the deployed LLM Manager service into `startupteams/llm-manager-project-framework`, bootstrap the repository with authoritative requirements/architecture/operations documentation, and make the deployed service reproducible without committing secrets or production data.\n\n## Features in Scope\n\n### Deployed service capture\n\n**Requirements**\n\n- `REQ-001`\n- `REQ-020`\n- `REQ-030`\n- `REQ-040`\n- `REQ-060`\n- `REQ-062`\n- `REQ-063`\n- `REQ-070`\n\n**Work items**\n\n- [ ] Inventory deployed VM114 source, services, configuration, runtime, database schema, and external dependencies.\n- [ ] Copy application source into the repository without secrets, venvs, model files, logs, or production database contents.\n- [ ] Capture sanitized service/unit/config examples and reproducible dependency/version information.\n- [ ] Add/verify repository validation commands.\n- [ ] Run syntax/tests available from the captured project.\n- [ ] Run a secret scan before commit.\n\n### Documentation bootstrap\n\n**Requirements**\n\n- `REQ-001` through `REQ-073` as applicable to documented scope.\n\n**Work items**\n\n- [ ] Add populated requirements, future work, architecture, operations, implementation status, ACMS integration, and model-serving docs.\n- [ ] Add accepted ADRs for human-directed architecture decisions.\n- [ ] Add TDRs for intentionally carried operational debt.\n- [ ] Update `AGENTS.md` with LLM Manager-specific safety rules.\n\n## Definition of Done\n\n- [ ] The deployed source needed to reproduce LLM Manager is represented in the repository.\n- [ ] No secret-bearing files or production database contents are committed.\n- [ ] The README explains the business reason and service boundary.\n- [ ] Approved requirements and acceptance criteria are recorded.\n- [ ] Future/unapproved work is separated from approved requirements.\n===", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

### [2026-09-29T08:19:06.068125+00:00] tool · tool: read_file

{"content": "1|# TDR-0008 — llmmanager DB ORM migration is staged, not complete\n2|\n3|Status: Accepted (REV4 §6 allows staged/behavior-preserving migration)\n4|\n5|## Context\n6|\n7|The llmmanager database has 20+ public tables + an agentmanager schema, all created by raw\n8|SQL (db/schema/schema.sql) and accessed by psycopg2 in the 2,600-line single-file app.\n9|Migrating all of it to SQLAlchemy ORM in one sprint risks regressions in the revenue-critical\n10|LLM serving path.\n11|\n12|## Decision\n13|\n14|- This sprint migrates ONLY the new agent_runtime_manager database to ORM+Alembic (greenfield).\n15|- LLM Manager machine API reads (model-routes, usage) go through SQLAlchemy Core `text()`\n16|  queries against the EXISTING schema — no schema change, no ORM models for those tables yet.\n17|- Existing write paths stay on the proven psycopg2 app code.\n18|- Follow-up sprints migrate tables group by group (deployments/hosts/model_registry first,\n19|  telemetry second) behind the adapter layer, each with row-count + checksum verification per REV4 §6.\n20|\n21|## Consequences\n22|\n23|- Two persistence styles coexist temporarily in the llmmanager DB (raw SQL + Core reads).\n24|- `server_manager.llm_manager.adapters` isolates the eventual cutover.\n25|", "total_lines": 24, "file_size": 1174, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

# 2026-09-29 — ACMS Real Dispatch, Recovery Learning, Agent-State Boundary, Jira Integration, and Speed-to-Lead External Architecture Plan

**Primary agent:** STEA-004 Entrepreneur  
**Date:** 2026-09-29  
**Purpose:** Continue from `FINAL-HANDOFF-CONTINUATION-20260928.md`. Prove the newly deployed budget-gated ACMS dispatch path against the real worker, implement the architecture directions that are now sufficiently clear, perform bounded technology spikes for unresolved agent-state/Jira/external-agent questions, and avoid inventing unresolved customer-product decisions.

---

```text
FUTURE_WORK.md
relevant ADRs/TDRs

```

# 4. Phase A — prove real budget-gated ACMS → Bridge → Hermes dispatch

This is the exact next action from the previous handoff.

## A1. Configure live Bridge targets

On CT122:

1. inspect current deployment/env conventions;
2. set `ACMS_BRIDGE_TARGETS_JSON` outside Git;
3. include only the intended test worker (`acms-worker-001` / VM124);
4. validate without printing secrets;
5. restart/redeploy only the required ACMS service;
6. verify `/health`, `/version`, and existing smoke checks.

Do not put Bridge credentials into Git, logs, screenshots, or handoff.

## A2. Create one bounded real Work Item

Use a harmless deterministic task.

Requirements:

```text
clear Work ID
clear acceptance criteria
small soft/hard budget
no destructive operations
no sensitive/customer data
no infrastructure change
```

## A3. Dispatch through the authoritative path

Use:

```text
POST /api/v1/dispatch/work/{work_item_id}
```

Prove:

```text
Work
→ budget execution-check
→ execution task
→ Bridge
→ Hermes
→ model execution
→ result
→ handoff/checkpoint
→ cost attribution
→ economics records
→ semantic events
```

## A4. Validate idempotency/accounting

Verify:

```text
retry does not create duplicate execution
model/provider/session linkage correct
token/cost records correlated
normal successful execution does not create spurious Attention
SSE reflects meaningful state changes
```

Use tests/mocks for hard-budget denial if a production denial probe would be wasteful.

## A5. Record proof

Capture:

```text
Work ID
Assignment ID
Execution Session ID
A2A/Bridge task ID
model/provider
cost/tokens
result
budget state
handoff reference
```

Checkpoint.

---

# 5. Phase B — codify measurable-goal Work lifecycle

Human direction from the merged workbook:

```text
measurable goal
→ execute
→ handoff
→ next measurable goal gets a new plan/fresh session
```

Current ACMS may still use multiple execution sessions inside one Work Item when needed.

## B1. Update architecture/requirements

Codify this **default policy** without removing flexibility:

```text
Feature / Program
  → Work Item = one measurable goal
      → one or more execution sessions if needed
      → complete handoff
  → next measurable goal = next child Work Item
```

A session crash, context rotation, or retry during the same goal does **not** automatically require a new Work Item.

If an ADR decision still needs human acceptance, keep it Proposed and make only additive/non-breaking changes.

## B2. Parent/child continuity

Inspect existing hierarchy first.

If not already supported, add the smallest safe representation needed to link sequential child Work Items to a parent Feature/Program.

Do not build a competing hierarchy.

## B3. Handoff → next work

Where existing Executive Agent authorization already permits it, support:

```text
completed Work + complete handoff
→ Executive Agent creates/proposes next child Work
→ same approved parent scope
→ provenance recorded
```

No silent scope expansion.

## B4. Review-loop guardrail documentation

Add an architecture rule:

```text
review findings = BLOCKING / REQUIRED / OPTIONAL
OPTIONAL findings do not prevent completion
review does not create new product scope
```

Do not yet invent a hard review-loop count unless repository requirements already define one.

Checkpoint.

---

# 6. Phase C — recovery learning and anti-infinite-loop behavior

The human has now given concrete runtime/work retry direction.

## C1. Runtime recovery

Policy:

```text
maximum 5 automatic recovery attempts
attempts should vary where possible
then Human Attention
```

Audit current ARM reconciler against this.

## C2. Persist recovery-method history

If missing, record at least:

```text
failure class/signature
attempt number
recovery method
timestamp
result
time-to-recovery
error
```

Prefer semantic/audit records over a second mutable history system if the existing event model is sufficient.

## C3. Strategy progression

Implement bounded strategy progression for failure classes actually supported by the current system.

Example only:

```text
observe/recheck
→ restart affected Bridge/harness
→ restart service/runtime
→ guest/VM restart
→ final safe recovery strategy
→ Human Attention
```

Do **not** automatically make PDU hard-cycle the fifth step.

## C4. Running without ACMS assignment

Human direction:

```text
do not automatically stop
```

Add/confirm an informational/warning semantic state:

```text
runtime RUNNING
primary ACMS assignment absent
```

No automatic remediation.

## C5. Work reassignment loop guard

Architecture direction:

```text
maximum 3 failed reassignments by Executive Agent
→ stop automatic reassignment
→ prominent Human Attention
```

Add data/audit support for:

```text
failed_work_count
reassignment_count
retry/reassignment reason
changed dimension
```

Do not automate the old subjective `<60% instruction match` rule.

Instead, document that failure should eventually be based on explicit acceptance criteria/evaluation.

Require a retry to record what changed, such as:

```text
different agent/model
revised instructions
additional context
different strategy
human clarification
```

Checkpoint.
